determine_primary_parameters

determine_primary_parameters

       This command determines the primary parameters based on sensitivity analysis, following a
       compute sensitivity task run_sensitivity_analysis() (see run_sensitivity_analysis on
       page 275). Primary parameters are defined as parameters with high sensitivity. Secondary
       parameters are defined as parameters with low sensitivity.

       Syntax

          determine_primary_parameters(parameters=<t>,
                                                              [num_parameters=<i>,]
                                                              [min_sensitivity=<f>,]
                                                              [use_mean=<b>,] [measure=<s>,]
                                                              [plot_file=<s>])

Argument         Type         Description
parameters       <t>
num_parameters   <i>          Specifies a list of calibration parameter objects for which a sensitivity
min_sensitivity  <f>          analysis has been performed previously.
use_mean         <b>
measure          <s>          Sets the maximum number of primary parameters. The minimum
                              number is 1. Default: 6
plot_file        <s>
                              Sets the sensitivity of only primary parameters, which must be greater
                              than min_sensitivity. Default: 0.001

                              Specifies whether to use the mean sensitivity of individual targets
                              instead of total sensitivity. Default: False

                              Specifies the sensitivity measure as follows:
                              · For local sensitivity analysis, specify 'absolute' or 'relative'

                                 (default).
                              · For global sensitivity analysis, specify 'mu', 'mu_star' (default),

                                 or 'sigma'.

                              Specifies the name of the PNG file of the sensitivity bar plot. If None,
                              then no plot is created. Default: None

Returns
<t>: List of primary parameter objects only

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide  237

W-2024.09
Appendix A: Commands
determine_primary_parameters_ml

       Examples

          cal.determine_primary_parameters(parameters=parameters,
                                                                      num_parameters=5,
                                                                      plot_file='sensitivity.png')

          cal.determine_primary_parameters(parameters=parameters,
                                                                      use_mean=True, measure='absolute')

determine_primary_parameters_ml

       This command determines the primary parameters based on sensitivity analysis, following a
       compute sensitivity task run_sensitivity_analysis_ml(). Primary parameters are
       defined as parameters with high sensitivity. Secondary parameters are defined as
       parameters with low sensitivity.

       Syntax

          determine_primary_parameters_ml(parameters=<t>,
                                                                    [task_name=<s>,]
                                                                    [child_dir=<s>,]
                                                                    [num_parameters=<i>,]
                                                                    [min_sensitivity=<f>,]
                                                                    [targets=<t>])

Argument    Type     Description
parameters  <t>
                     Specifies a list of calibration parameter objects for which a sensitivity
task_name   <s>      analysis has been performed previously.

child_dir   <s>      Sets the name of the task that is used in the earlier performed
                     run_sensitivity_analysis_ml(task_name=<s>).
num_parameters <i>   Default: "ML_sensitivity"

min_sensitivity <f>  Specifies the directory of the child projects. If set to None, then
                     option("child_dir") is used.
targets     <t>
                     Sets the maximum number of primary parameters. The minimum
                     number is 1. Default: 8

                     Sets the sensitivity of only primary parameters, which must be greater
                     than min_sensitivity. Default: 0.001

                     Specifies a list of targets to be included. Default: None

       Simple Evaluation on page 36.

       Syntax

          evaluate(parameters=<t>, targets=<t>, [parent=<s>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                          [extra_tool_parameters=<s|d>])

Argument               Type Description

                                 option("parent") is used.

child_dir              <s> Specifies the directory of the child projects. If set to None, then
                                 option("child_dir") is used.

extra_tool_parameters  <s|d>  Specifies extra tool parameters to be added to the child
                              project.

                              If set to None, then option("extra_tool_parameters") is
                              applied.

                              If still set to None (default), then no parameters are added.

                              If it specifies a string, then all parameters are added to this
                              tool.

                              If it specifies a dictionary, with tool labels as keys and lists of
                              parameter objects as values, then parameters are added to
                              individual tools.

                              Default: None

Appendix A: Commands
generate_pairplot

generate_pairplot

       This command as an epilogue generates a correlation pairplot with parameter or target data
       for commands specific to the mlcal calibration module (see Machine Learning Calibration
       Module: mlcal on page 52). It conducts three representative analyses.

       First, the distribution characteristics of the sample and the presence of outliers are checked
       through a histogram of the parameter or target data.

       In addition, through the scatter plot, you can check the shape of the target space and check
       the correlation between the two paired variables through regression fitting. To this end, the
       initial fitting model can be checked through options such as fit_order and
       confidence_interval.

       On the other hand, if multiwindow DoE is used, labels can be given for each DoE group and
       samples can be distinguished by color. Regression is performed for each DoE group.

       Only for scalar target data, Mahalanobis distance data is added to make you aware of the
       distance between samples considering covariance in the target space. Outliers observed in
       the histogram of this distance data mean that it is likely less trained in training. The formula
       is given by:

where x is the vector of the single observation (row in a dataset),  is the mean vector of all
samples, and  is the covariance matrix of targets.

Syntax

generate_pairplot(targets=<t>, [parameters=<t>,]
                                [combine_plots=<b>,][fit_regression=<b>,]
                                [fit_order=<i>,][confidence_interval=<i>,]
                                [outfile_prefix=<s>,][outfile_suffix=<s>])

Argument              Type  Description
targets               <t>
parameters            <t>   Specifies a list of targets.
combine_plots         <b>
                            Specifies a list of parameters.
fit_regression        <b>
fit_order             <i>   If False, separate pairplots for parameters and targets
                            are generated, respectively. If True, a combined
                            pairplot is generated additionally. Default: False

                            Specifies whether to estimate and to plot a regression
                            model relating the x and y variables. Default: True

                            Order of regression fitting. Default: 1

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                                         248

merge_tables

merge_tables

       This epilogue command merges two Legendre polynomial implantation model (L-PIM)
       tables in the procal calibration module (see Legendre Polynomial Implantation Model
       Calibration on page 109).

       Syntax

          merge_tables(table=<s>, table_damage=<s>, [table_2=<s>,]
                                 [table_damage_2=<s>,] [table_dir=<s>,]
                                 [regularize=<b>,] [interpolator=<k>]

Argument              Type  Description
table                 <s>   Specifies the base L-PIM dopant table.
table_damage          <s>   Specifies the base L-PIM damage table.
table_2               <s>   Specifies the added L-PIM dopant table. If None, the table from the
table_damage_2        <s>   previous task is taken. Default: None
table_dir             <s>   Specifies the added L-PIM damage table. If None, table from the
regularize            <b>   previous task is taken. Default: None
                            Specifies the directory of output L-PIM tables. Default: "./PIM"
interpolator          <k>   Specifies whether to regularize the grid table. If True, add virtual points
                            for regular grid table. If False, keep unstructured grid table. Default:
                            True
                            Specifies the Interpolator: "RGLI" for Regular Grid Linear Interpolator
                            (default), "RBF" for Radial Basis Function

Returns
None

Examples

 procal.merge_tables(table='../PIM/nitrogen_in_siliconcarbide.pim',
            table_damage='../PIM/nitrogen_damage_in_siliconcarbide.pim')

Appendix A: Commands
Model

Model

       This class represents a machine learning model.

       Syntax

          ml_0 = Model(name=<s>, hyperparameters=<d>, framework=<k>,
                               conditions=<d>, load=<b>, validate=<b>)

Argument              Type  Description
name                  <s>
                            The name of the model. This is the name used during automatic file
hyperparameters       <d>   saving and loading.

                            Sets the hyperparameters for the Model object. For the Mx1
                            framework, the dictionary requires entries for each target name (for
                            example, {"Target 1": ..., "Target 2": ..., }). For the MxN
                            framework, use "All" (for example, {"All": ...})
                            All models have the following possible hyperparameter entries:
                            · model_type <k>: Specifies the machine learning model type
                            · model_hyperparameters <d>: Specifies the hyperparameters for

                               the machine learning model selected in model_type. These
                               hyperparameters are unique to each model.
                            · parameter_scaler <k>: Specifies the scaling to apply to input
                               parameters
                            · target_scaler <k>: Specifies the scaling to apply to output
                               targets
                            · feature_interaction_type <k>: Specifies the type of feature
                               interaction terms to be considered
                            · feature_interaction_params <d>: Specifies the feature
                               interaction settings
                            · feature_selection_type <k>: Specifies the type of feature
                               selection algorithm to use
                            · feature_selection_parameters <d>: Specifies the feature
                               selection settings
                            · retrain <b>: Specifies whether to retrain the model every time
                               train_ml is called
                            · encoder_type <k>: Specifies the type of encoder to use for profile
                               targets
                            · encoder_params <d>: Specifies the profile encoder settings
                            · optimizer_type <k>: Specifies the optimizer to use when
                               optimizing model_hyperparameters
                            · optimizer_params <d>: Specifies the hyperparameter optimizer
                               settings

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                            254

W-2024.09
Appendix A: Commands
Model

Argument              Type  Description

framework             <k>   The specifics for each entry can be quite detailed and depend on the
condition             <d>   specifics of each model. See Model Hyperparameters on page 54 for
load                  <b>   more details on how to set up a hyperparameter dictionary for your
validate              <b>   specific case. Default: None

                            Sets which model framework to use. Options are Mx1 or MxN. High
                            dimensional targets can be used only with the Mx1 framework.
                            Default: Mx1

                            Specifies conditional parameter of the model. Default: None.

                            Specifies whether to load an existing model or not. If True, it searches
                            <name>_untrained.ml or <name>_trained.ml files under the project
                            directory while creating an instance. Default: False

                            Specifies whether to validate attributes of the Model while loading an
                            existing model. Default: True

Returns

An object of type Model

Examples

 ml_0 = Model(name="ml_0", hyperparameters={"Y0": {"model_type": MLPR,
                         "model_hyperparameters": {"hidden_layer_sizes": [[64, 64,
                         64], [128,128,128]], "alpha": [0.1, 0.3, 1.0], "max_iter':
                         [5000]}, "optimizer_type": GridSearch, "optimizer_params":
                         {"cv": 10}}}, framework=Mx1, validate=True, load=False)

 ml_1 = Model(name="ml_1_test", hyperparameters={"profile_1":
                         {"model_type": MLPR, "model_hyperparameters":
                         {"hidden_layer_sizes": [256, 256, 256], "alpha": 1.0},
                         "parameter_scaler": MinMax, "target_scaler": Identity,
                         "encoder_type": PointSamplingEncoder, "encoder_params":

                         framework=Mx1, conditions={"cond1": "cond_value"}
                         validate=False, load=True)

optimize

optimize

       This command performs the gradient-based local calibration task.

       Syntax

          optimize(parameters=<t>, targets=<t>, [parent=<s>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                          [max_iteration=<i>])

       Syntax for pmccal Module

          optimize(parameters=<t>, targets=<t>, [parent=<s>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                          [max_iteration=<i>,] [reevaluate=<b>])

Argument    Type Description

parameters  <t> Specifies a list of calibration parameter objects.

targets     <t> Specifies a list of calibration target objects.

parent      <s> Specifies the global parent project. If set to None, then
                      option("parent") is used.

child_dir   <s> Specifies the directory of the child projects. If set to None, then
                      option("child_dir") is used.

max_iteration <i>     Sets a limit to the maximum number of iterations for the calibration.
                      Default: None, which specifies to set to max_iteration in
                      set_option()

reevaluate  <b> (For pmccal module only) Specifies whether to reevaluate by rerunning
                      the simulation due to variability. Default: True

Returns
None
Examples

 cal.optimize(parameters=[p1, p2, p3], targets=[t1, t2, t3])

Appendix A: Commands
Parameter

Parameter

       This class represents a model parameter to be calibrated.

       General Syntax

          Parameter(name=<s>, value=<f>, minimum=<f>, maximum=<f>,
                            [transform=<o>,] [choices=<t>,] [sort=<b>,] [type=<k>,]
                            [dtype=<k>,] [precision_rel=<i>,]
                            [precision_abs=<i>,] [force_pass=<b>])

Argument    Type      Description
name        <s>       Specifies the name of a calibration parameter. This must be the name of
                      a Sentaurus Workbench parameter to be calibrated.
value       <f>       Specifies the initial value or the default of the parameter.
minimum     <f>       Specifies the minimum-allowed value for the parameter.
maximum     <f>       Specifies the maximum-allowed value for the parameter.
transform   <o>       Specifies the transform object for the parameter value. The transformed
                      value is "transform(value)". The predefined transformers are
choices     <t>       Linear, Log10, Log, Square, Neg, and Sqrt (see Table 2 on page 23).
                      Specifies the candidate values for a discrete parameter.
sort        <b>       Specifies whether to sort the discrete parameter values before
                      encoding. Default: False
type        <k>       Specifies an optional type for parameter classification.
                      Available types for the igbtcal module are IcVg, IcVc, BV, CV, Qg, and
dtype       <k>       Switching.
                      Specifies the Python datatype of the parameter. Options are bool,
precision_rel <i>     float, int, or str. Default: float
                      Specifies the relative precision given as a number of significant digits.
precision_abs <i>     Default: 8
                      Specifies the absolute precision given as a number of digits after the
force_pass  <b>       decimal point (or before the decimal point if it is negative).
                      (Discrete parameters only) Specifies whether samples are forced to
                      pass all the values given by choices. Default: False

Returns

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                                         257

W-2024.09
Appendix A: Commands
Parameter

       <o>: Parameter object
       Examples

          Parameter(name="p1", value=1.0, minimum=-10, maximum=10)

          Parameter(name="p2", value=1e18, minimum=1e15, maximum=1e21,
                            transform=Log10)

          Parameter(name="p1", value=2.0, minimum=1.5, maximum=2.5, type=IcVg)

          Parameter(name="p2", value=1.0, minimum=1E-4, maximum=1E4,
                            transform=Log10, type=IcVc)

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                                    258

Parent

Parent

       This class represents a parent project to be used for calibration.

       Syntax

          Parent(name=<s>, path=<s|o>, tools=<t>, [experiment=<i>,]
                    [files_to_copy=<t>,] [extra_tool_parameters=<s|d>])

Argument              Type Description

name                  <s>    Specifies the name of a parent project.

path                  <s|o>  Specifies the path (as string or Path object) to the Sentaurus
                             Workbench project.

tools                 <t>    Specifies the list of tools in the parent project tool flow.

experiment            <i>    Sets the number of the nominal experiment of the parent project.

                             Default: 1

files_to_copy         <t>    Specifies the Sentaurus Workbench tool steps to be included in the

                             calibration. Default: None

extra_tool_parameters <s|d>  Specifies the extra tool parameters to be added to the child project.
                             If set to None (default), then no parameters are added.
                             If it specifies a string, then all parameters are added to this tool.
                             If it specifies a dictionary, with tool labels as keys and lists of parameter
                             objects as values, then parameters are added to individual tools.
                             Default: None

Returns
<o>: Parent object

Examples

 Parent(name = "myparent", path = "../parent/myparent",
            tools = ['sprocess'], files_to_copy = ['./calib.fps'],
            extra_tool_parameters = 'sprocess')

Appendix A: Commands
plot_data

plot_data

       As a task epilogue, this command acts as the plot function for mlcal tasks. The supported
       plot types are curve, hist, line, parallel_coordinates, scatter, and surface plots.
       The generated graphs are based on two different backends, mpl (for matplotlib), and
       plotly. The mpl produces static images, while the plotly generates interactive graphs,
       which allow user interaction.

       After completion of an mlcal task, the graph is generated using the internal data frame
       containing parameters and targets data and helps to analyze the results.

       Syntax

          plot_data(targets=<t>, parameters=<t>, x=<s|t>, y=<s|t>, [z=<s>,]
                            [plot_type=<k>,][backend=<k>,][width=<i>,][height=<i>,]
                            [y_axis=<k>,][reference_data=<t>,][reference_data=<t>,]
                            [outfile_prefix=<s>,][outfile_suffix=<s>,][**kwargs=<d>])

Argument              Type Description
targets
parameters            <t> Specifies the list of targets.
x
y                     <t> Specifies the list of parameters.
z
plot_type             <s|t> Specifies the x variable or the list of x variables.

backend               <s|t> Specifies the y variable or the list of y variables.
width
height                <s> Specifies the z variable. Default: None

                      <k> Selects the type of visualization. Options are:
                                 · "curve": Curve plot for profile target.
                                 · "hist": Histogram plot for single variable.
                                 · "line": Line plot with x and y variables.
                                 · "parallel_coordinates": Parallel coordinate plot for more than
                                    two variables only for "plotly" backend.
                                 · "scatter": Scatter plot with x and y variables.
                                 · "surface": Surface plot with x, y and z variables.
                                Default: "scatter".

                      <k> Selects the backend between "plotly" and "mpl" (matplotlib).
                                Default: "plotly"

                      <i> Figure width. Default: 800

                      <i> Figure height. Default: 600

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide  260

                            Y-axis setting. Options are:
reference_data <t>          · "dual": Dual y-axes plot.
                            · "multi": Subplots with rows (number of y variables) and cols
reference_label <t>
                               (number of x variables).
outfile_prefix <s>          · "None": Single y-axis plot (Default).

predict_ml

       This command returns the prediction from one or more trained machine-learning models,
       specific to the mlcal calibration module (see Machine Learning Calibration Module: mlcal
       on page 52).

       Syntax

          predict_ml(models=<t>, parameters=<t>, targets=<t>,
                              [parent=<s>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                              [task_name=<s>,] [doe_method=<k>,]
                              [doe_size=<i|t|d>,] [doe=<s|p>,]
                              [check_boundary=<b>,] [check_adaptive=<b>,]
                              [save_report=<b>,] [save_report_figures=<b>])

Argument              Type     Description
models                <t>      Specifies a list of trained machine-learning model objects.
parameters            <t>      Specifies a list of machine-learning model parameter objects.
targets               <t>      Specifies a list of machine-learning model target objects.
parent                <s>      Specifies the global parent project. If set to None, then
child_dir                      option("parent") is used.
task_name             <s>      Specifies the directory of the child projects. If set to None, then
doe_method                     option("child_dir") is used.
                      <s>      Sets the name of the machine-learning task. Default:
doe_size                       "ML_predict"

                      <k>      Sets the DoE method. Options are "box-behnken",
                      <i|t|d>  "full-factorial", "gaussian", "grid",
                               "latin-hypercube", "sobol", "uniform" ,
                               "model_train_data", "model_validate_data", and
                               "model_data".
                               Default: "model_data"

                               Sets the number of samples for the DoE in the case of the
                               DoE methods "latin-hypercube", "uniform", and
                               "sobol".

                               Sets the number of samples per parameter in the case of the
                               DoE method "grid", which is either the same for all in the
                               case of an integer or per parameter in the case of a list or
                               dictionary.

reverse_search_ml

       This command performs a reverse search analysis, specific to the mlcal calibration module
       (see Machine Learning Calibration Module: mlcal on page 52).

       Syntax

          reverse_search_ml(models=<t>, parameters=<t>, targets=<t>,
                                          [parent=<s>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                                          [task_name=<s>,] [num_candidates=<i>,]
                                          [doe_method=<k>,] [doe_size=<i|t|d>,] [doe=<s|p>,]
                                          [range=<s|p>,] [cluster=<s>,] [optimize=<b>,]
                                          [enable_parameter_filter=<b>,]
                                          [extract_search_result=<b>,]
                                          [return_output=<b>,]
                                          [save_report=<b>,] [priority_targets=<t>,]
                                          [update_parameters=<s>,] [reset_results=<b>,]
                                          [args_nsgaIII=<d>])

Argument                    Type              Description
models                      <t>               Specifies a list of machine-learning models. If more than
                                              one model is provided, then reverse search is combined.
parameters                  <t>               Specifies a list of machine-learning model parameter
                                              objects.
targets                     <t>               Specifies a list of machine-learning model target objects.
parent                      <s>               Specifies the global parent project. If set to None, then
                                              option("parent") is used.

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                                                    271

W-2024.09

reverse_search_ml
                          <s>                 Specifies the directory of the child projects. If set to None,
         Argument
          child_dir                           then option("child_dir") is used.
          task_name
          num_candidates  <s>                 Sets the name of the machine-learning task.
          doe_method
                                              Default: "ML_reverse"

                          <i>                 Sets the number of search results to store. Default: 1
          doe
          range           <k>                 Sets the DoE method. Options are "adaptive",
          cluster
                                              "box_behnken", "full_factorial", "gaussian",
          optimize                            "grid", "latin_hypercube", "sobol", "uniform",
                                              "NSGAIII_S" (non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm
                                              for multi-modal optimization), and "NSGAIII_M" (NSGAIII
                                              for multi-objective optimization). Default: "uniform".

                          <i|t|d>             Sets the number of samples for the DoE in the case of the
                                              DoE methods "gaussian", "latin-hypercube",
                                              "uniform", and "sobol".

                                              Sets the number of samples per parameter in the case of
                                              the DoE method "grid", which is either the same for all in
                                              the case of an integer or per parameter in the case of a list
                                              or dictionary.

                                              Default: 1000

                                              the specified doe. Default: None

                                              frame. Parameter ranges are updated based on the
                                              provided range. Default: None

                          <s>                 Applies K-Means clustering.

                                              · "optimal_number": Finds optimal cluster number by
                                                 using silhouette score and elbow method.

                                              · "fixed_number": Clusters the search results into
                                                 "num_candidates" number of clusters.

                                              · "off": Turns off clustering.
                                              Default: "off"

W-2024.09
Appendix A: Commands
search

search

       This command performs an evaluation of different designs-of-experiments (DoE) to explore
       the parameter space.

       Syntax

          search(parameters=<t>, targets=<t>, method=<s>,
                      [options=<d>,] [parent=<s>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                      [include_points=<t>,] [include_nominal=<b>])

Argument              Type  Description
parameters            <t>
targets               <t>   Specifies a list of calibration parameter objects.
method                <s>
                            Specifies a list of calibration target objects.

                            Sets the method used to perform the search (see
                            Design-of-Experiments Search on page 39). Options are:
                            · "bayesian_optimizer" creates experiments adaptively, starting

                               from an initial set of experiments defined by Latin hypercube
                               sampling. In each iteration, the algorithm selects some parameter
                               sets that have the greatest potential to minimize the response.
                            · "grid" creates experiments with parameter values defined on a
                               regular grid.
                            · "latin" creates experiments with parameter values using Latin
                               hypercube sampling.
                            · "uniform" creates experiments with random parameter values.
                            · "sobol" creates experiments with parameter values using a Sobol
                               sequence.
                            · "turbo" creates experiments adaptively and builds on top of
                               Bayesian optimization by keeping separate models that can
                               change the focus of their search as the algorithm progresses. This
                               makes it more likely to converge to a better local minimum.
                            · The name of a Sentaurus Workbench DoE creates experiments
                               according to the given Sentaurus Workbench DoE.
                            · "MOEAD", "NSGAII", and "NSGAIII" create experiments adaptively
                               and are multi-objective evolutionary algorithms, which use
                               evolutionary methods to solve the optimization problem. For details
                               about these algorithms, see SentaurusTM Workbench Optimization
                               Framework User Guide, Multi-Objective Optimization.
                            Some of these methods have additional options that can be specified
                            using the options argument. For details about these options, see
                            SentaurusTM Workbench Optimization Framework User Guide,
                            Searching the Parameter Space.

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide  279

W-2024.09
Appendix A: Commands
search

Argument              Type  Description
options               <d>   Specifies a dictionary of specific options for the search method.
parent                <s>   Specifies the global parent project. If set to None, then
                            option("parent") is used.
child_dir             <s>   Specifies the directory of the child projects. If set to None, then
                            option("child_dir") is used.
include_points <t>          Specifies a list of dictionaries defining points to be included in the
                            search. Default: None
include_nominal <b>         Specifies whether to include the current nominal in the search.
                            Default: True

Returns

None

Examples

 cal.search(parameters=[p1, p2], targets=[t1, t2], method="grid",
                     options={"Ns": (2, 2), "midpoint": True, "boundary": True})

 cal.search(parameters=parameters, targets=targets, method="sobol",
                     options={"Ns": 10})

 cal.search(parameters=parameters, targets=targets,
                     method="bayesian_optimizer",
                     options={"Ns": 5, "max_iter": 3})

 cal.search(parameters=parameters, targets=targets,
                     method="turbo",
                     options={"Ns": 15, "boundary": True, "trust_regions": 3,
                     "batch_size": 5, "max_iter": 15, "disp": 1})

 cal.search(parameters=parameters, targets=targets,
                     method="NSGAII",
                     options={"termination": {"n_gen": 50}, "pop_size": 40,
                     "n_offsprings": 10, "disp": True})

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                                                        280

set_backend_control

       This command defines the control parameters for the calibration backend.

       Syntax

          set_backend_control([initdx=<f>,] [sensdx=<f>,]
                                              [mindx=<f>,] [maxdx=<f>,]
                                              [ssq_error_tolerance=<f>,]
                                              [parameter_tolerance=<f>,]
                                              [target_tolerance=<f>,] [jacobian_options=<d>,]
                                              [method=<s>,] [options=<d>])

Argument              Type  Description
initdx                <f>
                            Sets the initial increment of the normalized value for the
sensdx                <f>   calibration by the scalib backend. Default: 4e-2

mindx                 <f>   Sets the increment of the normalized value for sensitivity
                            analysis. Default: 0.1
maxdx                 <f>
                            Sets the minimum increment of the normalized value for the
ssq_error_tolerance <f>     Jacobian calculation by the scalib backend. Default: 1e-7

parameter_tolerance <f>     Sets the maximum increment of the normalized value for the
                            Jacobian calculation by the scalib backend. Default: 1.0
target_tolerance      <f>
                            Sets the tolerance of the normalized update of the sum of
jacobian_options      <d>   squares (ssq) error by the scalib backend. Default: 1e-5

method                <s>   Sets the tolerance of the normalized parameter update by the
                            scalib backend, or sets the absolute tolerance for the backends
                            "least_squares", "minimize", and "mkl_nls". Default: 2e-3

                            Sets the tolerance of the total target error. Default: 0.0

                            Specifies a dictionary of keyword arguments passed to the
                            Jacobian evaluation for backends other than the scalib
                            backend.
                            For details, see SentaurusTM Workbench Optimization
                            Framework User Guide, Method-Specific Optimization Options.

                            Sets the calibration method used by backends other than the
                            scalib backend.
                            For details, see SentaurusTM Workbench Optimization
                            Framework User Guide, Method-Specific Optimization Options.

set_option

set_option

       This command sets user-specified option values. The options listed here are general ones
       available for all calibration modules.

       Note:
           The set_option() command must be written using the format
           <argument>=<value>. This command does not accept value-only input.

       Syntax

          set_option([attempt_failed=<b>,]
                              [check_child_project=<b>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                              [extra_tool_parameters=<s|d>,] [files_to_copy=<t>,]
                              [max_iteration=<i>,] [ml_num_points_of_encoder=<i>,]
                              [num_threads=<i>,] [parent=<s>,]
                              [parent_experiment=<i>,] [preprocess_new_project=<b>,]
                              [queue=<s>,] [run_limits=<d>,] [runtime_limits=<d>,]
                              [scheduler=<s>,] [split_child=<b>,]
                              [split_child_max_experiments=<i>,]
                              [submit_bridge_tool=<b>,] [submit_virtual=<b>,]
                              [tools=<t>,] [use_scalib_weights=<b>])

Argument               Type Description
attempt_failed
check_child_project    <b> Specifies whether to re-submit failed and terminated
                                 nodes (for example, in restarts). Default: True
child_dir
error_precision        <b> Specifies whether to check the child project consistency
extra_tool_parameters            by running an initial experiment after creating the child
                                 project. Default: False

                       <s> Specifies the child project directory. Default: "../child/"

                       <i> Specifies the internal error precision. Default: 8

                       <s|d>  Specifies the extra tool parameters to be added to the
                              child project.

                              If set to None (default), then no parameters are added.

                              If it specifies a string, then all parameters are added to this
                              tool.

                              If it specifies a dictionary, with tool labels as keys and lists
                              of parameter objects as values, then parameters are
                              added to individual tools.

                              Default: None

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                                      284

W-2024.09
Appendix A: Commands
set_option

Appendix A: Commands
Target

Target

       This class represents a model target against which it is calibrated. The target can be a scalar
       or an xy profile.

       Syntax for Scalar Targets

          Target(name=<s>, value=<f>,
                      [id=<s>,] [type=<k>,] [dtype=<k>,] [conditions=<d>,]
                      [weight=<f>,] [scale=<f>,] [penalty=<o>,] [range=<t>,]
                      [abs_value_range=<t>,] [values=<t>,]
                      [dimension=<i>,] [error_method=<s>,] [parent=<o>])

Argument              Type  Description
name                  <s>
                            Specifies the name of a Sentaurus Workbench parameter or variable
value                 <f>   in which the simulation response will be available (as a scalar value).

id                    <s>   Specifies the value to be matched by the simulation response. It must
type                  <k>   be a single scalar value. Default: 0.0

dtype                 <k>   Specifies a unique name or an ID of the target.

conditions            <d>   Specifies the type for target classification.
                            Available types for the igbtcal module are IcVg, IcVc, BV, CV, Qg,
weight                <f>   and Switching.

scale                 <f>   Specifies the datatype of the target. Options are bool, float, or int.

penalty               <o>   Specifies the condition or split parameters that must be set to
                            calculate the calibration target.
range                 <t>
                            Specifies the weight to be applied to the calibration target.
abs_value_range <t>         Default: 1.0

                            Sets the scaling factor used to normalize the target value, with
                            value_scaled = value / scale.
                            Default: 1.0

                            Specifies the penalty to be applied to the target in case of violation of
                            the constraints. It must be of type TargetPenalty (see TargetPenalty
                            on page 296). Default: None

                            Sets the constraint range [low, high] into which the passing target
                            value falls. One of the bound can be None.

                            Sets the range [low, high] into which the absolute value of the passing
                            target falls. One of the bound can be None.

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide  288

W-2024.09
Appendix A: Commands
Target

Argument              Type  Description
values                <t>
dimension             <i>   Specifies a list of passing discrete target values.
error_method          <s>
                            Specifies the dimension of a high-dimensional (> 2) target.
parent
                            Specifies the method used to calculate the pointwise error between
                            the simulation response and the reference value. Options are:
                            · "abs": Absolute error: ei = outi - targeti
                            · "log_abs": Absolute logarithmic error:

                                ei = log10 outi - log10 targeti
                                                                                                log10 outi - log10 targeti

                            · "log_rel": Relative logarithmic error: ei = --------------------------------------------------------------

                            · "rel": Relative error: ei = -------------------------------

                                                                             targeti
                            · "signed": Signed error: ei = outi - targeti

                                                                                                        outi - targeti
                            · "signed_relative": Signed relative error: ei = -------------------------------

                                                                                                            targeti
                            · "square": Squared error: ei = outi - targeti2

                                The default applies as follows:
                                 · "abs" for type=IcVc or type=Qg or type=Switching
                                 · "log_abs" for type=IcVg or type=CV
                                 · "rel" for type=None or type=BV

                      <o> Specifies the parent project of the target. If None, the global parent
                                specified in the options is used. Default: None

TargetDelta

       This class represents a delta target, which can be used as calibration target for the
       difference (the delta) between two scalar targets.

       Syntax

          TargetPenalty(id=<s>, value=<f>, targets=<t>, [weight=<f>])

Argument   Type  Description
id         <s>
value      <f>   Specifies a unique name or an ID of the target.

targets    <t>   Specifies the value of the difference to be matched by the simulation
weight     <f>   response. It must be a single scalar value.

                 Specifies a list of two targets to be used for the delta calculation.

                 Specifies the weight to be applied to the calibration target.
                 Default: 1.0

Returns
<o>: TargetDelta object

Examples

 t1_BottomZ = Target(name="BottomZ", value=2.0,
                                      conditions={"time": "10"})

 t2_BottomZ = Target(name="BottomZ", value=3.0,
                                      conditions={"time": "20"})

 t_BottomZ_Delta = TargetDelta(id="t_BottomZ_Delta", value=1.0,
                                                        targets= [t2_BottomZ, t1_BottomZ],
                                                        weight=2.0)

          def calculate_distance(targets):
               a = targets[0]
               b = targets[1]
               distance = a.eval - b.eval
               return distance

Appendix A: Commands
TargetPenalty

TargetPenalty

       This class represents a function which can be used for the penalty argument of Target
       objects (see Target on page 288). If the constraint defined in TargetPenalty is not
       satisfied, then a penalty is applied to the target.

       Syntax

          TargetPenalty(filters=<t>, penalty=<c>, [ignore_value=<b>])

Argument              Type  Description
filters               <t>
penalty               <c>   Specifies a list of tuples defining the feasible region.

ignore_value          <b>   Specifies a callable penalty function that calculates a penalty, given
                            the difference between the target and the threshold of the constraint.

                            Specifies whether to ignore the actual value of the target, keeping
                            only the penalty. Default: False

Returns
<o>: TargetPenalty object

Examples

 def quadratic_penalty(delta):
         return delta**2

 TargetPenalty([("lt", 1.0), ("gt", 0.5)], quadratic_penalty)

 TargetPenalty([("lt", 1.0)], quadratic_penalty)

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                                                  296

Appendix A: Commands
Targetr2

Targetr2

       This class represents an r2 target. r2 is the square of the Pearson correlation coefficient (r),
       which is a linear correlation between the reference values and the evaluated values. (It is not
       a coefficient of determination.) For details, see r2 Targets on page 26.

       Syntax

          Targetr2(id=<s>, value=<f>, targets=<t>, [weight=<f>])

Argument              Type  Description
id                    <s>   Specifies a unique name or an ID of the target.
value                 <f>   Specifies the value to be matched by the simulation response. It must
                            be a single scalar value.
targets               <t>   Specifies a list of targets to be used during the r2 calculation.
weight                <f>   Specifies the weight to be applied to the calibration target.
                            Default: 1.0

Returns
<o>: Targetr2 object

Examples

 t1_BottomZ = Target(name="BottomZ", value=2.0,
                                      conditions={"time": "10"})

 t2_BottomZ = Target(name="BottomZ", value=3.0,
                                      conditions={"time": "20"})

 t3_BottomZ = Target(name="BottomZ", value=4.0,
                                      conditions={"time": "30"})

 t_BottomZ_r2 = Targetr2(id="t_BottomZ_r2", value=1.0,
                                      targets = [t1_BottomZ, t2_BottomZ, t3_BottomZ],
                                      weight=1.0)

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide                                           297

train_ml

       This command trains one or more untrained machine-learning models, specific to the mlcal
       calibration module (see Machine Learning Calibration Module: mlcal on page 52).

       Syntax

          train_ml(models=<t>, parameters=<t>, targets=<t>,
                          [parent=<s>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                          [task_name=<s>,] [save_report=<b>,]
                          [retrain=<b>,] [get_cv_result=<b>,] [data_set=<s>])

Argument              Type  Description
models                <t>   Specifies a list of untrained machine-learning model objects.
parameters            <t>   Specifies a list of machine-learning model parameter objects. A
                            parameter defined in the machine-learning model during creation and
targets               <t>   not defined in this list is excluded from the model during training.
parent                <s>   Specifies a list of machine-learning model target objects.
child_dir             <s>   Specifies the global parent project. If set to None, then
task_name             <s>   option("parent") is used.
save_report           <b>   Specifies the directory of the child projects. If set to None, then
retrain               <b>   option("child_dir") is used.
                            Sets the name of the machine-learning task. Default: "ML_train"
get_cv_result         <b>   Specifies whether to save a report in the report folder. Default: True
                            Specifies whether to discard existing training results to retrain a new
                            model if applicable. Default: False
                            Specifies whether to save output images from cross-references.
                            Default: False

SentaurusTM Calibration Workbench User Guide  299

W-2024.09
Appendix A: Commands
train_ml

Argument              Type  Description
data_set              <s>
                            Specifies the subset of data on which the model is trained. Options
                            are:
                            · "full": Entire dataset
                            · "passed": Subset that passes the target criteria before data

                               generation
                            · "passedtrimmed": Subset that passes both target criteria and data

                               trimming criteria
                            · "trimmed": Subset that passes the data trimming criteria after data

                               generation
                            Default: "full"

Returns

None

Examples

 mlcal.train_ml(models=models, parameters=parameters,
                             targets=targets)

 mlcal.train_ml(
       models=models,
       parameters=parameters,
       targets=targets,
       task_name='ML_train',
       save_report=True,
       retrain=True,
       get_cv_result=True,
       data_set='passedtrimmed',

 )

validate_range_ml

       This command validates the parameter ranges and adjusts them for subsequent tasks.

       Syntax

          validate_range_ml(parameters=<t>, targets=<t>,
                                          [parent=<s>,] [child_dir=<s>,]
                                          [task_name=<s>,]
                                          [num_validations=<i>,]
                                          [reducing_ratio=<f>,]
                                          [constraint=<s>,]
                                          [num_retries=<i>,]
                                          [num_evaluations=<i>])

Argument              Type  Description
parameters            <t>   Specifies a list of calibration parameter objects.
targets               <t>   Specifies a list of calibration target objects.
parent                <s>   Specifies the global parent project. If set to None, then
                            option("parent") is used.
child_dir             <s>   Specifies the directory of the child projects. If set to None, then
                            option("child_dir") is used.
task_name             <s>   Sets the task name. Default: "ML_validate_range"
num_validations       <i>   Sets the number of validations to adjust the range. Default: 13
reducing_ratio        <f>   Specifies the ratio used to reduce the parameter range. Default: 0.2
constraint            <s>   Specifies an optional constraint or query for an extraction variable of
                            simulations to be included in the training dataset. Default: ""
num_retries           <i>   Sets the number of simulation run retries if there is a simulation
                            runtime failure. Default: 1
num_evaluations       <i>   Sets the number of initial evaluations. Default: 1

Returns
None
Examples

 cal.validate_range_ml(parameters=parameters, targets=targets)
